2610	  /** GlowMask 叠画：与基础精灵同一变换（调用点在 drawEnemy 的 save 块内）。
2611	   *  bx/by = 基础精灵在本变换内的目标左上（与本体严格同锚——地面型底锚/飞行型
2612	   *  中心锚由调用方传入，各层不再自行假设锚点）。
2613	   *  additive（XNA 默认 AlphaBlend + 贴图自带发光色的语义，用 lighter 还原
2614	   *  "叠加提亮"观感） */
2615	  private drawNpcGlow(
2616	    e: Enemy,
2617	    r: { img: CanvasImageSource; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number },
2618	    bx: number, by: number,
2619	  ) {
2620	    if (!this.atlas) return;
2621	    const g = Renderer.NPC_GLOW[e.vanillaId ?? -1];
2622	    if (!g) return;
2623	    const img = this.atlas.ensureVImage(g.tex);   // 懒加载（首帧 null，次帧生效）
2624	    if (!img) return;
2625	    const ctx = this.ctx;
2626	    const frames = Math.max(1, e.vanilla?.frames ?? 1);
2627	    const scale = (e.vanilla?.scale ?? 1) * e.vanillaScale;
2628	    const alphaRaw = Math.min(255, Math.max(e.spawnAlpha, 0));
2629	    const opacity = 1 - alphaRaw / 255; // NPC.Opacity
2630	    // Glow 表与 NPC 表同布局（同宽 × 帧数行）；帧行 = 当前帧（原版一律 rCurrentNPC.frame）
2631	    const gh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(img.height / frames));
2632	    const row = Math.min(frames - 1, Math.max(0, Math.floor(r.sy / Math.max(1, r.sh))));
2633	    const sy = Math.min(img.height - gh, row * gh);
2634	    ctx.save();
2635	    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
2636	    // 第三批通用 alpha 表达式（g.a）：数字=常数 / '255a'=(255-α)/255 / '128a2'=(128-α/2)/255
2637	    // / 'opacity'=GetAlpha(White)。缺省（旧两批）走下方 id 链。
2638	    const aExpr = g.a;
2639	    const resolveAlpha = (): number => {
2640	      if (aExpr === undefined) return -1; // 哨兵：走旧链
2641	      if (typeof aExpr === 'number') return aExpr;
2642	      if (aExpr === '255a') return (255 - alphaRaw) / 255;
2643	      if (aExpr === '128a2') return (128 - alphaRaw / 2) / 255;
2644	      return opacity;
2645	    };
2646	    if (g.mode === 'frame4') {
2647	      // frame4：中心整图（a 表达式）+ 四向偏移 4 份灰 A=64
2648	      //（Main.cs:25639-25649/25671-25681/25700-25710/25720-25731；速度偏移 num =
2649	      // k + 光照项（恒 k），或 UnitX.RotatedBy 固定 px :25736/:25746）
2650	      const offX = (g.dx ?? 0) / scale;   // 518 halfSize+(-10,0) → 图像右移 10 世界 px
2651	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, resolveAlpha()));
2652	      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, sy, img.width, gh, bx + offX, by, r.sw, r.sh);
2653	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (64 / 255) * opacity));
2654	      // 416 骑手速度恒 0（AI_075 吸附零速）——原版取坐骑 415 的速度
2655	      // （Main.cs:24516 spinningpoint3 = npc[415].velocity；同 vel4 分支）
2656	      const svx = e.vanillaId === 416 && e.master && !e.master.dead ? e.master.vx : e.vx;
2657	      const svy = e.vanillaId === 416 && e.master && !e.master.dead ? e.master.vy : e.vy;
2658	      const fixed = g.fixed4;
2659	      const mult = g.velMult ?? 0.5;
2660	      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
2661	        const ang = k * Math.PI / 2;
2662	        const dx = (fixed !== undefined
2663	          ? Math.cos(ang) * fixed                       // UnitX.RotatedBy(k*π/2)×n
2664	          : (svx * Math.cos(ang) - svy * Math.sin(ang)) * mult) / scale;
2665	        const dy = (fixed !== undefined
2666	          ? Math.sin(ang) * fixed
2667	          : (svx * Math.sin(ang) + svy * Math.cos(ang)) * mult) / scale;
2668	        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, sy, img.width, gh, bx + offX + dx, by + dy, r.sw, r.sh);
2669	      }
2670	    } else if (g.mode === 'frame') {
2671	      let alpha = 0.8;
2672	      let flashPulse = 1;
2673	      if (e.vanillaId === 551) { alpha = (66 / 255) * 1.3; }              // :23099 A=66 ×(0.7+0.3*lerp)
2674	      else if (e.vanillaId === 564 || e.vanillaId === 565) { alpha = 0.5 * opacity; } // :23484 white.A/2×Opacity
2675	      else if (e.vanillaId === 548) {
2676	        // :23590-23596 num63/65 三秒呼吸三角波 ×0.6 紫底（(140,50,255)）
2677	        const t3 = (performance.now() / 1000) % 3 / 3;
2678	        flashPulse = t3 > 0.5 ? 1 - t3 : t3;
2679	        alpha = 0.6 * Math.max(0, flashPulse);
2680	      } else if (e.vanillaId === 399) { alpha = (127 - alphaRaw / 2) / 255; }  // :24568 Color(127-α/2,…)
2681	      else if (e.vanillaId === 421) { alpha = (128 - alphaRaw / 2) / 255; }    // :25622 Color(128-α/2,…)
2682	      else alpha = 0.78;                                                  // Color(200,200,200,0)/白色系通用
2683	      const ga = resolveAlpha();
2684	      if (ga >= 0) alpha = ga;                                            // 第三批表项覆盖旧链
2685	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha));
2686	      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, sy, img.width, gh, bx, by, r.sw, r.sh);
2687	      // 399 探测器状态灯（:24571-24575）：Extra_20 按 ai0+1 取行，画在本体下方 +24px，
2688	      // 再叠 Glow_101 同源矩形罩（A=127-α/2）
2689	      if (g.lamp) {
2690	        const lamp = this.atlas.ensureVImage(g.lamp.tex);
2691	        const lampGlow = this.atlas.ensureVImage(g.lamp.glow);
2692	        if (lamp && lampGlow) {
2693	          const rows = 4, lh = lamp.height / rows;
2694	          const lrow = Math.max(0, Math.min(rows - 1, Math.floor(e.ai0) + 1)); // Frame(1,4,0,ai0+1)
2695	          const lx = -lamp.width / 2;                    // 水平居中（原版 X=本体 drawPos.X=中心）
2696	          const ly = by + g.lamp.dy / scale;             // 世界 px +24 → 局部除 scale
2697	          ctx.globalAlpha = opacity;                     // GetAlpha(npcColor)
2698	          ctx.drawImage(lamp, 0, lrow * lh, lamp.width, lh, lx, ly, lamp.width, lh);
2699	          ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (127 - alphaRaw / 2) / 255));
2700	          ctx.drawImage(lampGlow, 0, lrow * lh, lampGlow.width, lh, lx, ly, lamp.width, lh);
2701	        }
2702	      }
2703	    } else if (g.mode === 'vel4') {
2704	      // vel4：velocity.RotatedBy(k*π/2) × num 偏移画 4 份灰 A=64（:23750/:24535）
2705	      const num = g.velMult ?? 4; // (GetAlpha-0.5).Length()*k 光照项恒 0（我们无逐 NPC 光照）
2706	      // 416 骑手速度恒 0（AI_075 吸附零速）——原版此处取坐骑 415 的速度
2707	      // （Main.cs:24516 spinningpoint3 = npc[415].velocity）
2708	      const svx = e.vanillaId === 416 && e.master && !e.master.dead ? e.master.vx : e.vx;
2709	      const svy = e.vanillaId === 416 && e.master && !e.master.dead ? e.master.vy : e.vy;
2710	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (64 / 255) * opacity));
2711	      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
2712	        const a = k * Math.PI / 2;
2713	        // 世界坐标偏移 → 本变换局部（当前原点=精灵锚点，除 scale 还原）
2714	        const dx = ((svx * Math.cos(a) - svy * Math.sin(a)) * num) / scale;
2715	        const dy = ((svx * Math.sin(a) + svy * Math.cos(a)) * num) / scale
2716	          + (e.vanillaId === 517 ? 2 : 0);
2717	        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, sy, img.width, gh, bx + dx, by + dy, r.sw, r.sh);
2718	      }
2719	    } else {
2720	      // trail：419 火月怪（:25677-25696 本体罩 + 分支四向残影；:24610-24645 拖尾）
2721	      // 本体罩 Color(255-α,…) → 全亮白×Opacity
2722	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, opacity));
2723	      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, sy, img.width, gh, bx, by, r.sw, r.sh);
2724	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (64 / 255) * opacity));
2725	      if (e.ai2 >= -6) {

2829	      }
2830	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

2838	    ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, -r.sw / 2, -r.sh / 2, r.sw, r.sh);
2839	    ctx.restore();
2840	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
2841	  }

// ---- sparkle 彩虹（Main.hslToRgb，Main.cs:47266-47290 1:1）----
function hue2rgb(v1: number, v2: number, vH: number): number {
  if (vH < 0) vH += 1;
  if (vH > 1) vH -= 1;
  if (6 * vH < 1) return v1 + (v2 - v1) * 6 * vH;
  if (2 * vH < 1) return v2;
  if (3 * vH < 1) return v1 + (v2 - v1) * ((2 / 3) - vH) * 6;
  return v1;
}
/** Main.hslToRgb 1:1（GetShimmerGlitterColor :766-771 以 s=1/l=0.5 调用）→ RGB 0-1 */
function hslToRgb(hue: number, sat: number, lum: number): [number, number, number] {
  if (sat === 0) return [lum, lum, lum];
  const v2 = lum < 0.5 ? lum * (1 + sat) : lum + sat - lum * sat;
  const v1 = 2 * lum - v2;
  return [hue2rgb(v1, v2, hue + 1 / 3), hue2rgb(v1, v2, hue), hue2rgb(v1, v2, hue - 1 / 3)];
}

// ---- sparkle 染色变体缓存（离线预渲染）----
// 关键①：sparkle 闪纹是灰度像素（饱和度 0），CSS hue-rotate 对纯白/纯灰是 no-op——
// 旧实现 ctx.filter=hue-rotate 等于没上色，闪纹显示为白色而非原版彩虹。
// 故离线预渲染染色副本：hue 量化 16 档（((px+py/6)+t/30)/6 % 1），每档一条

	public static float GetLerpValue(float from, float to, float t, bool clamped = false)
	{
		if (clamped)
		{
			if (from < to)
			{
				if (t < from)
				{
					return 0f;
				}
				if (t > to)
				{
					return 1f;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (t < to)
				{
					return 1f;
				}
				if (t > from)
				{
					return 0f;
				}
			}
		}
		return (t - from) / (to - from);
	}

	public static float Remap(float fromValue, float fromMin, float fromMax, float toMin, float toMax, bool clamped = true)
	{
		return MathHelper.Lerp(toMin, toMax, GetLerpValue(fromMin, fromMax, fromValue, clamped));
	}

	public static double Remap(double fromValue, double fromMin, double fromMax, double toMin, double toMax, bool clamped = true)
	{
		return Lerp(toMin, toMax, GetLerpValue(fromMin, fromMax, fromValue, clamped));
	}

	{

	}

But the A=num220 destination attenuation (significant) → destination-out with alpha num220/255 first. Careful ordering: destination-out then lighter add of the tinted sprite. So vector62_local = (bx + r.sw/2, by + r.sh/2). Therefore sparkle center local = (bx + r.sw/2 + 27/scale, by + r.sh/2).

Draw Extra_98 centered at that point: drawImage(ex, 0,0,ex.width,ex.height, cx - w/2×s, cy - h/2×s, w×s, h×s) where s = the num-modulated scale and rotation applied via save/translate/rotate.

Scales (vanilla): 
- pair A (green, rot π/2+0): vector = (fatness.X×0.5, scale.X)×num = (2×0.5, 3)×num = (1, 3)×num — canvas scale (1×num, 3×num) with rotation π/2.
- pair B (green→color2? no): second draw = same color (shineColor) at rotation 0+0 with vector2 = (fatness.Y×0.5, scale.Y)×num = (1, 1.5)×num.
- pair C: color2 (white×0.5) rot π/2, scale = vector×0.6 = (0.6×num, 1.8×num).
- pair D: color2 rot 0, scale = vector2×0.6 = (0.6×num, 0.9×num).

Brightness: color = Green×opacity×0.5×num → additive green at brightness 0.5×opacity×num (tint green via... Green = (0,128,0). Multiplying the grayscale sparkle texture by green → only G channel survives → a green streak. Implement via tintedSprite with rgb (0,128,0)? tintedSprite multiplies → result (0, g×128/255, 0) — matches XNA multiply. Then globalAlpha = 0.5×opacity×num... wait: color = shineColor × opacity × 0.5 then ×= num. In XNA premult-style: added light = texel×color.rgb. Our canvas lighter adds texel.rgb×texel.a×globalAlpha. Using the tinted sprite (rgb pre-multiplied by (0,128,0)/255 and alpha = original): globalAlpha should = 0.5×opacity×num... but the canvas lighter also multiplies by texel alpha — XNA adds texel.rgb×color.rgb regardless of texel alpha (premult assumption). Extra_98: meanA 41, mostly semi-transparent — a soft glow star. Canvas 'lighter' will add less in transparent regions, XNA (premult textures? Terraria textures via FromStream are NON-premultiplied, so XNA out = texel.rgb×color.rgb + dst×(1-src.a×color.a)... hmm with srcBlend=One the texel.rgb is added unpremultiplied — for non-premult textures, transparent regions still add their rgb (usually black or stale). 

This level of precision is beyond reasonable; the repo's established convention (lighter + globalAlpha) is accepted for glows. I'll follow it: tinted sprite + globalAlpha = 0.5×opacity×num for the green pair, and untinted with globalAlpha = 0.5×num for the white pair (color2 = white×0.5×num).

Hmm wait — color2 = drawColor × 0.5 where drawColor = Color(255,255,255,0) → (127.5,127.5,127.5,0); then ×= num → brightness 0.5×num. ✓.

Now the 425 sparkle gate: ai3 (vanilla ai[3] for Storm Diver) — what is it semantically? In AI_003 fighters... ai[3] is often unused or a timer. Whatever — driven by AI; if our AI never sets ai3, envelope = Remap(0,...)=0 → invisible. Hmm — that would make the sparkle never show. Let me check what vanilla 425's ai[3] does... ```
So 425's ai[3] is the "aim charge" counter: 0→30 while having line-of-sight (sparkle grows), fires at 30 (laser shotgun), then counts 31→40 (fadeOut window 30→36!), resets to 30−180 = −150 and climbs back to 0.

So the sparkle envelope maps exactly: num224 = ai3:
- opacity = Remap(ai3, 0, 15, 0, 1) → grows during aim charge.
- num = GetLerpValue(0,30,ai3) × GetLerpValue(36,30,ai3) → full at 30, fades 30→40 (the post-fire window). At ai3 ≤ 0 → num = 0 (GetLerpValue(0,30,0)=0). 

So the sparkle is invisible when ai3 ≤ 0 (the −150..0 cooldown). Our AI for 425 (AI_003 family) — does it implement this ai3 counter? Let me check the repo's AI_003 for 425. If not, the sparkle won't show. Let me grep 425 in the repo AI.

center: 0.1 },\n  };\n\n  /** Utils.GetLerpValue 1:1（Utils.cs:283-308，含 from>to 反向钳位） */\n  private static glowLerpValue(from: number, to: number, t: number): number {\n    if (from < to) { if (t < from) return 0; if (t > to) return 1; }\n    else { if (t < to) return 1; if (t > from) return 0; }\n    return (t - from) / (to - from);\n  }\n\n  /** 轨道族逐帧参数（Main.cs:25826-25829 num299/num300/num302 初值 + 各 case 覆写 +\n   *  :26091/:26108/:26123 半径公式）。tSec=GlobalTimeWrappedHourly；返回 null=不画\n   *  （568/569 出生门未过）。ai2 仅 661 消费，且按原版 Clamp(0,50)（NPC.cs:45253）钳界。 */\n  static npcGlowOrbitParams(\n    id: number, tSec: number, ai2: number,\n  ): { count: number; num300: number; radius: number; phase: number } | null {\n    const c = Renderer.NPC_GLOW_ORBIT[id];\n    if (!c) return null;\n    if (c.spawnGate) {\n      // :26116 rCurrentNPC.localAI[3] >= 60f 门——本仓 AI_107(ImprovedWalkers) 未承载\n      // 出生计时（lai3 恒 0），drawNpcGlow 按出生完成态 60 代入（出生 3 份紫雾收敛\n      // :25899-25907 属本体绘制层，未接，另登记）\n    }\n    const x = tSec % c.period / c.period * Math.PI * 2;\n    let num300 = c.env === 'half' ? Math.cos(x) / 2 + 0.5 : Math.cos(x) / 6 + 0.75;\n    const a2 = Math.max(0, Math.min(50, ai2));\n    let phase = 0;\n    if (c.ai2Gate) num300 = Math.max(num300, Renderer.glowLerpValue(0, 60, a2));\n    if (c.phaseAi2) phase = 3 * Renderer.glowLerpValue(0, 60, a2);\n    const base = c.ai2Radius ? c.rBase + a2 : c.rBase;\n    return { count: c.count, num300, radius: base * num300 + c.rAdd, phase };\n  }\n\n  /** 轨道拷贝染色（color43 MultiplyRGBA，RGB 0..255）：\n   *  - gold：Gold(255,215,0)（:26087/:25914）\n   *  - hotpink：HotPink(255,105,180)，源色 A 通道为 0 → 乘后 A 恒 0（:25895-25896）\n   *  - hsl：661 逐份 hslToRgb((GT+i/count)%1, 1, 0.5)（:26105，Main.cs:47266-47290），\n   *    hue 量化 1/32 防逐帧打爆 tintCache */\n  static npcGlowOrbitTint(id: number, i: number, tSec: number): [number, number, number] {\n    const c = Renderer.NPC_GLOW_ORBIT[id];\n    if (c?.tint === 'gold') return [255, 215, 0];\n    if (c?.tint === 'hotpink') return [255, 105, 180];\n    if (c?.tint === 'hsl' && c) {\n      const hq = Math.round(((tSec + i / c.count) % 1) * 32) / 32;\n      const [r, g, b] = Renderer.glowHslToRgb(hq % 1, 1, 0.5);\n      return [Math.round(r * 255), Math.round(g * 255), Math.round(b * 255)];\n    }\n    return [255, 255, 255];\n  }\n\n  /** Main.hslToRgb 1:1（Main.cs:47266-47290）→ RGB 0..1（s=0 灰阶捷径同源码） */\n  private static glowHslToRgb(hue: number, sat: number, lum: number): [number, number, number] {\n    if (sat === 0) return [lum, lum, lum];\n    const v2 = lum < 0.5 ? lum * (1 + sat) : lum + sat - lum * sat;\n    const v1 = 2 * lum - v2;\n    const h2r = (h: number): number => {\n      if (h < 0) h += 1;\n      if (h > 1) h -= 1;\n      if (6 * h < 1) return v1 + (v2 - v1) * 6 * h;\n      if (2 * h < 1) return v2;\n      if (3 * h < 1) return v1 + (v2 - v1) * (2 / 3 - h) * 6;\n      return v1;\n    };\n    return [h2r(hue + 1 / 3), h2r(hue), h2r(hue - 1 / 3)];\n  }\n\n  /** 414 爬足虫尾受击红闪（Main.cs:25656-25663）：localAI[2]≠0 时 color38 覆写为\n   *  (255-num219, num219, num219, 255-num219)；num219 = trunc(lai2)，负段 = 128+trunc。\n   *  返回 null = 无闪（走基础 255a 白罩）。A = 255-num219 ∈ 127..255 → 显著，调用方\n   *  需做 fadeA 背景衰减。localAI[2] 的递减驱动在 AI_006（NPC.cs:52026-52047：\n   *  受击置 128，每 tick −16，过零翻 −128 后 +16 回升）——本仓 412-414 无 AI 承载。 */\n  static npcGlowCrawltipedeFlash(lai2: number): { rgb: [number, number, number]; a: number } | null {\n    if (lai2 === 0) return null;\n    let n = Math.trunc(lai2);\n    if (lai2 < 0) n = 128 + Math.trunc(lai2);\n    const inv = 255 - n;\n    return { rgb: [inv, n, n], a: inv };\n  }\n\n  /** 387 特斯拉炮塔充能白化（Main.cs:25451-25458）：color32 = White×0.75（RGB=A=191）；\n   *  ai0>0 时 amount7=(ai0+1)/60 → RGB Lerp(0.75,1)、A Lerp(191,0)，再整体 ×Opacity。\n   *  bright=加色亮度（RGB/255），fadeA=背景衰减（A/255，静止 191/255 显著）。\n   *  本仓 teslaTurretAI 的 ai0 即充能计数（0→60，受击 -30）→ 分支实时触发。 */\n  static npcGlowTeslaFade(ai0: number, alphaRaw: number): { bright: number; fadeA: number } {\n    const opacity = (255 - Math.min(255, Math.max(alphaRaw, 0))) / 255;\n    const amount7 = ai0 > 0 ? (ai0 + 1) / 60 : 0;\n    const rgb = (191 + (255 - 191) * amount7) / 255;\n    const a = 191 * (1 - amount7) / 255 * opacity;\n    return { bright: rgb * opacity, fadeA: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, a)) };\n  }\n\n  /** 425 枪口星芒包络（Main.cs:25631-25633 + DrawPrettyStarSparkle :34741-34745）：\n   *  opacity = Remap(ai3, 0, 15, 0, 1)（瞄准蓄力渐亮）；\n   *  num = GetLerpValue(0,30,ai3) × GetLerpValue(36,30,ai3)（ai3≤0 冷却期恒 0，\n   *  30 满蓄、30→40 放射后渐灭——与 NPC.cs:58986-59006 的 ai3 状态机同环）。 */\n  static npcGlowSparkleEnvelope(ai3: number): { opacity: number; num: number } {\n    const o = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ai3 / 15));\n    const num = Renderer.glowLerpValue(0, 30, ai3) * Renderer.glowLerpValue(36, 30, ai3);\n    return { opacity: o, num: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, num)) };\n  }\n\n  /** 160/209 城镇 NPC 微光/派对变体贴图档（Main.cs:25769/:25783）：\n   *  - 160 松露人：townNpcVariationIndex==1（微光态）→ Glow_352，否则 Glow_166\n   *  - 209 电子人：常态 Glow_167；微光态按 altTexture 选 Glow_350（≠1）/Glow_351（==1）\n   *  返回 null = 该 id 无变体链。【登记】城镇 NPC 走 drawTownNPC（TownNPC 类），\n   *  不经过 drawNpcGlow → 本链当前仅作 1:1 备案，接通需 drawTownNPC 侧挂 glow 层\n   *  （该函数非本批所有权范围）；160 的 shimmerTransparency RGB 衰减（:25771-25776）\n   *  TownNPC 未承载该字段，亦登记。 */\n  static npcGlowVariantTex(id: number, shimmered: boolean, altTexture: number): string | null {\n    if (id === 160) return shimmered ? 'vanilla/Glow_352.png' : 'vanilla/Glow_166.png';\n    if (id === 209) return shimmered ? (altTexture !== 1 ? 'vanilla/Glow_350.png' : 'vanilla/Glow_351.png') : 'vanilla/Glow_167.png';\n    return null;\n  }"
}

Our ai2 ∈ [0,180]. radius = (2+min(ai2,50))×num300+2 → at ai2=50: 52×num300+2 ∈ [2,54]. num302 = 3×(50/60)=2.5. The vanilla max-state (despawning lacewing) does look like a big expanding rainbow ring — so worst case our port shows the "despawning" visual constantly. The HSL hue 0.5 gives (0,0,255) blue — my test expectation of cyan was wrong. Terraria's hslToRgb: `c = hue2rgb(hue + 1/3, ...)` → R; `c2 = hue2rgb(hue)` → G; `num3 = hue2rgb(hue - 1/3)` → B. At hue=0.5: 
- R: hue2rgb(0.5+1/3=0.8333): 6×0.8333=5 ≥1; 2×0.8333=1.67≥1; 3×0.8333=2.5≥1 → return v1 = 2×0.5−v2. v2 = lum+sat−lum×sat = 0.5+1−0.5 = 1. v1 = 1−1 = 0 → R=0.
- G: hue2rgb(0.5): 6×0.5=3≥1; 2×0.5=1 not <1; 3×0.5=1.5≥1 → v1 = 0 → G=0.
- B: hue2rgb(0.5−1/3=0.1667): 6×0.1667=1.0 not <1 (exactly 1) → 2×0.1667=0.333<1 → v2=1 → B=1.
So (0,0,255) blue. Standard HSL gives cyan at 0.5, but Terraria's channel mapping (R=+1/3, G=hue, B=−1/3) puts blue at 0.5. So blue is correct per source. My test expectation was wrong; the implementation matches the decompiled formula. Fix the test to expect [0,0,255].

Let me double check the red at hue 0: 
- R: hue2rgb(1/3): 6×0.333=2≥1; 2×0.333=0.667<1 → v2=1 → R=1 ✓
- G: hue2rgb(0): 6×0=0<1 → v1+(v2−v1)×0 = v1 = 2×0.5−1 = 0 → G=0 ✓
- B: hue2rgb(−1/3 → +1/3 after wrap... wait: hue−1/3 = −0.333 → h<0 → h+=1 → 0.667: 6×0.667=4≥1; 2×=1.33≥1; 3×=2≥1 → v1=0 → B=0 ✓
So (255,0,0) red ✓ (that assertion passed).

### 3. Color.A 背景衰减审计
关键结论（XNA `BlendState.AlphaBlend`=src **One**，仓内已有先例 :2307-2310）：`out = texel·RGB + D·(1−texelA·A/255)`——**A 只削底不压加色亮度**。
- **显著（已实现 destination-out+lighter）**：437 A=200（上轮把 200/255 当加色亮度是错的，已改 a:1+fadeA）；387 A=191；414 闪段 A=255-n∈127..255
